# Metacells data

A gene expression matrix corresponding to the single cell object, in
this case it corresponds to the reduced single cell object (metacells),
not the original.

## Usage

``` r
metacells_data
```

## Format

Matrix with samples as columns and genes names (SYMBOL) as rows

## Source

Senosain et al. (2023), doi: 10.1158/2767-9764.CRC-22-0373. PMID:
37501683; PMCID: PMC10370362

## Examples

``` r
data(metacells_data)
```
